公司簡介
COMPANY PROFILE
農夫山泉股份有限公司成立於1996年，為 Established in 1996, Nongfu Spring Co., Ltd. is a leader in the
中國包裝飲用水及飲料的龍頭企業。我們 packaged drinking water and beverage industry in the PRC. Our
的產品主要覆蓋包裝飲用水、茶飲料、功能 major products include packaged drinking water, tea beverage,
飲料、果汁飲料及農產品等類別。本公司於 functional beverage, juice beverage and agricultural products. The
2001年6月從有限責任公司整體變更為股份 Company was transformed from a limited liability company into a
有限公司，並於2020年9月在香港聯交所主 joint stock company with limited liabilities in June 2001 and listed on
板上市（股份代號：9633.HK）。 the Main Board of the Hong Kong Stock Exchange in September
2020 (stock code: 9633. HK).
我們秉持「天然、健康」的產品理念，堅持使 We believe in offering natural and healthy products and insist on
用優質原料。我們的包裝飲用水全部源自優 using high quality raw materials. All of our packaged drinking water
質天然水源。我們擁有領先的裝備水平、製 products are sourced from quality natural water sources. We
造能力、強大的供應鏈管理能力和嚴格的質 possess industry-leading equipment, manufacturing capabilities,
量保障體系。 strong supply chain management capabilities and rigorous quality
assurance systems.
我們建立了包裝飲用水和飲料的雙引擎發展 We have established dual growth engines, packaged drinking water
格局，擁有覆蓋各省市及縣級行政區域的全 and beverage and set up a nationwide sales network covering all
國性銷售網絡。我們實行多品牌戰略，具有 provinces, municipalities and prefecture-level administrative divisions
長遠的品牌規劃和強大的品牌傳播能力，鑄 across China. We implement a multi-brand strategy, have long-term
就了中國軟飲料的超級品牌。 visions for our brands and strong brand communication capabilities,
and have built a super brand in the PRC soft beverage market.
2
農夫山泉股份有限公司 NONGFU SPRING CO., LTD. 年報 2025 Annual Report

並自2025年12月1日起施行，標誌著我國在 compliance requirements. In September 2025, the amendment
食品安全監管方面邁出了重要一步。此次修 to the Food Safety Law of the People’s Republic of China was
訂重點聚焦液態食品道路散裝運輸和嬰幼兒 passed and became effective from December 1, 2025, marking a
配方液態乳監管兩個突出問題，從強化准入 significant step forward in China’s food safety supervision. Focusing
管理、加強過程監管、完善法律責任等方面 on two major issues including bulk road transportation of liquid
作了有針對性的修改完善，有利於加強液態 food and supervision of liquid infant formula milk, it introduced
食品散裝運輸資質、運輸記錄與容器清洗憑 targeted modifications and improvements for strengthening access
證管理，實現從生產到消費全程可追溯。本 management, enhancing process supervision, and improving legal
次修訂為相關領域食品安全工作夯實制度基 responsibilities, which is conducive to strengthen the management of
礎，提供更好的法治保障，確保人民群眾 qualifications, transport records, and container cleaning certificates
「舌尖上的安全」。在食品行業監督管理進一 for the bulk transport of liquid food, enabling end-to-end traceability
步呈現體系化、全鏈條、精準化背景下，具 from production to consumption. This revision lays a solid institutional
備扎實研發能力、精準供應鏈管控及前瞻性 foundation for food safety in related fields, provides better legal
合規佈局的企業競爭優勢將更為凸顯。 guarantees, and ensures the people’s “safety of food intake”. In
this context of increasingly systematic, end-to-end, and targeted
supervision in the food industry, enterprises with robust R&D
capabilities, precise supply chain management, and forward-looking
compliance planning are better positioned to thrive and stand out
from the competition.

百萬元，較2024年的人民幣24,916百萬元 million, representing an increase of 27.7% as compared with
增加27.7%。報告期內本集團毛利率則由上 RMB24,916 million in 2024. During the Reporting Period, the

致力於成為行業可持續發展先鋒和引領者。 concept of sustainable development while providing natural and
展望未來，我們將繼續秉持「飲水思源」的責 healthy products. Looking ahead, we will continue to uphold the
任擔當，在奮力實現自身可持續發展目標的 responsibility embodied in “thinking of the source when drinking
同時，與各利益相關方攜手同行創造協同共 water.” While striving to achieve our own sustainable development
進，助力行業高質量發展與可持續繁榮。 goals, we will work hand in hand with all stakeholders to create
collaborative value and contribute to the high-quality development
and sustainable prosperity of the industry.

董事會已按照《企業管治守則》第二部分第 The Board has established a risk management and internal control
D.2段的要求，及參照COSO 內部控制框 system in accordance with the requirements of paragraph D.2 under
架、企業風險管理框架建立了風險管理及內 Section II of the Corporate Governance Code and COSO Internal
部監控系統，負責對本公司的風險管理及內 Control Framework and Corporate Risk Management Framework,
部監控系統的充分性和有效性進行獨立審 which is responsible for independently reviewing the adequacy and
核，持續監督並每年至少檢討一次其運行的 effectiveness of the risk management and internal control system
有效性。該系統旨在管理而非消除未能達成 of the Company, and continuously monitoring and reviewing the
經營目標的風險，促進有效及高效運營，合 effectiveness of its operation once a year. The system aims to
理保證財務報告可靠性及遵守適用法律及法 manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business
規以及保障本集團資產。董事會只能就不會 objectives, promote effective and efficient operations, reasonably
有重大的失實陳述或損失作出合理而非絕對 ensure the reliability of financial reports and comply with applicable
的保證。 laws and regulations and protect the assets of the Group. The Board
can only give reasonable but not absolute assurance that there will
be no material misrepresentation or loss.

舉報政策 WHISTLEBLOWING POLICY
本集團已設立舉報政策和系統，公開發佈了 The Group has established the whistleblowing policy and system,
舉報專用電話和郵箱，設有舉報人獎勵政 publicly released the special phone number and E-mail address
策，鼓勵舉報人參與到本集團廉潔體系中， for whistleblowing. A policy for awarding whistleblowers is in place
積極舉報貪污、腐敗等違法違紀行為和其他 to encourage them to participate in the Group’s integrity system
違反本公司規章制度的行為。本集團強調對 and actively report embezzlement, corruption, and other illegal and
舉報人的保護，對舉報人的個人信息及舉報 disciplinary violations, as well as other breaches of the Company’s
人提供的資料均嚴格保密，對惡意打擊報復 rules and regulations. The Group emphasizes the protection for
舉報人員的行為，將追究相關人員責任。所 whistleblowers. The personal information of whistleblowers and
接獲的舉報線索，由本集團審計稽查中心獨 the information provided by them shall be kept strictly confidential.
立開展第三方調查工作，確保過程和結果客 Any act of malicious retaliation against whistleblowers will be held

報其工作成果。本集團舉報政策詳情可於本 of the Group will independently conduct the third-party investigation
公司網站查閱。 on the reported clues to ensure the objectivity and impartiality of the
process and results. The audit and inspection center will report its
work results to the Audit Committee on a regular basis. Particulars of
the whistleblowing policy of the Group are available for inspection on
the website of the Company.

態度，我們通過《農夫山泉員工商業行為準 tolerance towards bribery and corruption. We have adopted the
則》、《員工獎懲管理辦法》等制度，對員工 “Code of Business Conduct for Employees of Nongfu Spring”, the
弄虛作假、貪污腐敗等違法行為和處罰標準 “Management Measures for Employee Rewards and Punishments”

業，致力於向消費者提供高質量的產品。本 beverage business in the PRC with devotion to providing consumers

夫山泉都有它的源頭」。本集團的包裝飲用 the Group has adhered to the principle that “every drop of Nongfu
水產品全部源自天然水源。我們已在中國各 Spring has its source” (每一滴農夫山泉都有它的源頭). The Group
不同區域佈局了十七大優質水源地，全面覆 sources all of packaged drinking water products from natural water

先後次序撥入各項基金）後的餘額）。 the statutory surplus reserve and, if any, the discretionary common
reserve (in such order of priorities)).

本公司的可供分派利潤為其根據(i)中國會計 determining distributable profit, the distributable profit of the Company
準則及規則；及(ii)國際或者境外上市地會計 shall be the lower of its profit after tax determined in accordance
準則計算所得的稅後利潤兩者中較低者。 with: (i) the PRC accounting standards and regulations; and (ii) the
international standards or the accounting standards of the place(s)
outside the PRC where the shares of the Company are listed.

人民共和國水法》、《建設項目環境保護管理 of the People’s Republic of China, the Environmental Impact
條例》等環境保護相關法律法規。 Assessment Law of the People’s Republic of China, the Water
Law of the People’s Republic of China, and the Regulations on the
Environmental Protection Management of Construction Projects (建
設項目環境保護管理條例) and other laws and regulations.
為了保護水源地環境，與水源地自然生態相 For the protection of the water source environment and the
和諧，本集團盡力減小對周邊環境的影響。 harmonization with the natural habitat of the water source, the Group
並且，本集團對生產及運營中的水資源、包 has made best efforts to minimize the impact on the surrounding
裝材料及能源使用情況、排污及碳排放情況 environment. In addition, the Group conducts specific monitoring
進行專門監察及管理，嚴格管控生產運營中 and management of water resources, packaging materials and
的環境生態影響。同時，本集團建立和不斷 energy usage, sewage and carbon emissions during the production
完善內部環境保護及可持續發展管理制度， and operations, and strictly controls the environmental and
通過構建良好的工廠健康、安全與環境管理 ecological impacts during the production and operations. Meanwhile,
體系及秩序，實現對工廠水資源、能源以及 the Group has established and been continuously improving the
三廢排放的全面管理，提升整體環境績效表 internal environmental protection and sustainable development
現。 management system. Through building a good health, safety and
environmental management system and order in the factory, the

1. 公司及集團資料 1. CORPORATE AND GROUP INFORMATION
農夫山泉股份有限公司於2001年6月 Nongfu Spring Co., Ltd. was incorporated and registered in the
27日在中華人民共和國（「中國」）註冊 People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”) on 27 June 2001. On 8
成立及登記。於2020年9月8日，本公 September 2020, the Company was listed on the Main Board
司於香港聯合交易所有限公司（「香港 of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “HKSE”)
聯交所」）主板上市（股份代號：9633. (stock code: 9633. HK). The address of the registered office is
HK）。註冊辦事處地址為浙江省杭州 No. 181, Geyazhuang Road, Xihu District, Hangzhou, Zhejiang
市西湖區葛衙莊181號。 Province.
本集團參與以下主要業務： The Group was involved in the following principal activities:
－ 生產及銷售包裝飲用水及飲料 – Production and sale of packaged water and beverage
－ 銷售農產品 – Sale of agricultural products
管理層認為，本公司的控股公司及最 In the opinion of management, the holding company and the
終控股公司為養生堂有限公司，該公 ultimate holding company of the Company is Yangshengtang
司於中國註冊成立。 Co., Ltd., which is incorporated and registered in the PRC.

（「國際會計準則」）及詮釋），以及香 International Accounting Standards Board (the “IASB”) and
港公司條例之披露規定編製。除按公 the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong Companies
平值計入損益的金融資產外，該等財 Ordinance. They have been prepared under the historical cost
務報表乃按歷史成本原則編製。除有 convention, except for financial assets at FVTPL which have
特別指明外，該等財務報表以人民幣 been measured at fair value. These financial statements are
（「人民幣」）呈列，且所有數值均約整 presented in Renminbi (“RMB”) and all values are rounded to
至最近的千元單位。 the nearest thousand except when otherwise indicated.

財務報表。 that it will continue to operate as a going concern.

司（統稱「本集團」）於截至2025年12月 statements of the Company and its subsidiaries (collectively
31日止年度的財務報表。附屬公司為 referred to as the “Group”) for the year ended 31 December
本公司直接或間接控制的實體（包括結 2025. A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity),
構性實體）。當本集團對參與被投資方 directly or indirectly, controlled by the Company. Control
業務的可變回報承擔風險或享有權利 is achieved when the Group is exposed, or has rights, to
以及能透過其權力影響被投資方的回 variable returns from its involvement with the investee and has
報時（即賦予本集團現有能力主導被投 the ability to affect those returns through its power over the
資方相關活動的既存權利），即取得控 investee (i.e., existing rights that give the Group the current
制權。 ability to direct the relevant activities of the investee).
於一般情況下均存在多數投票權形成 Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of voting
控制權之推定。倘本公司擁有少於被 rights results in control. When the Company has less than a
投資方過半數投票權或類似權利，則 majority of the voting or similar rights of an investee, the Group
本集團於評估其是否對被投資方擁有 considers all relevant facts and circumstances in assessing
權力時會考慮一切相關事實及情況， whether it has power over an investee, including:
包括：
(a) 與被投資方其他投票權持有人的 (a) the contractual arrangement with the other vote holders
合約安排； of the investee;
(b) 其他合約安排產生的權利；及 (b) rights arising from other contractual arrangements; and
(c) 本集團的投票權及潛在投票權。 (c) the Group’s voting rights and potential voting rights.

同的報告期間採用一致的會計政策編 for the same reporting period as the Company, using
製。附屬公司的業績自本集團取得控 consistent accounting policies. The results of subsidiaries
制權當日起綜合入賬，並繼續綜合入 are consolidated from the date on which the Group obtains
賬直至有關控制權終止當日為止。 control, and continue to be consolidated until the date that
such control ceases.

公平值計量 Fair value measurement
公平值為市場參與者於計量日期在有 Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset
序交易中出售資產所收取或轉讓負債 or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between
所支付的價格。公平值計量乃基於假 market participants at the measurement date. The fair value
設出售資產或轉讓負債的交易於資產 measurement is based on the presumption that the transaction
或負債的主要市場或（在無主要市場的 to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes place either in the
情況下）資產或負債的最有利市場進 principal market for the asset or liability, or in the absence of a
行。主要或最有利市場必須為本集團 principal market, in the most advantageous market for the asset
可進入的市場。資產或負債的公平值 or liability. The principal or the most advantageous market
乃採用市場參與者為資產或負債定價 must be accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset
時所用的假設計量，並假設市場參與 or a liability is measured using the assumptions that market
者依照彼等的最佳經濟利益行事。 participants would use when pricing the asset or liability,
assuming that market participants act in their economic best
interest.

扣的評估，並相應調整應付銷售折扣 rebates yearly and the sales discount and rebate payables are

本集團採用簡化法計提國際財務報告 The Group applies the simplified approach to providing for
準則第9號所規定的預期信貸虧損，其 expected credit losses prescribed by IFRS 9, which permits the
允許就所有貿易應收款項使用全期預 use of the lifetime expected credit loss provision for all trade
期信貸虧損撥備。 receivables.
應收票據為獲銀行於期限內無條件接 Bills receivable are bank acceptance bills that are
納的銀行承兌票據，且並不會就應收 unconditionally accepted by banks within the maturity period,
票據減值計提虧損撥備。 and there is no loss allowance for impairment of bills receivable.
年末使用撥備矩陣進行減值分析，以 An impairment analysis is performed at year end using a
計量貿易應收款項的預期信貸虧損。 provision matrix to measure expected credit losses for trade
撥備率乃基於具有類似虧損模式的多 receivables. The provision rates are based on days past due
個客戶分部組別的逾期日數釐定。該 for groupings of various customer segments with similar loss
計算反映了概率加權結果，貨幣時間 patterns. The calculation reflects the probability-weighted
價值以及年末可獲得的關於過往事 outcome, the time value of money and reasonable and
件、當前狀況及未來經濟狀況預測的 supportable information that is available at year end about past
合理及可支持的資料。 events, current conditions and forecasts of future economic
conditions.
當有資訊表明交易對手處於嚴重財務 The Group writes off trade receivables when there is
困難且並無實際收回可能，本集團將 information indicating that the counterparty is in severe financial
核銷其貿易應收款項。本集團亦在合 difficulties and there is no realistic prospect of recovery. The
適時考慮法律意見，例如交易對手已 Group also takes into account legal advice where appropriate,
被清盤或已進入破產程序，以較早者 e.g. when the counterparty has been placed under liquidation
為準。 or has entered into bankruptcy proceedings, whichever occurs
sooner.

31. 儲備 31. RESERVES
本集團的儲備金額及其變動於財務報 The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements
表的綜合權益變動表呈列。 therein are presented in the consolidated statement of changes
in equity in the financial statements.
(a) 法定儲備 (a) Statutory reserve
根據中華人民共和國公司法，中 In accordance with the Company Law of the People’s
國公司須將法定除稅後利潤的 Republic of China, the companies in the PRC are
10%轉撥至法定儲備，直至儲備 required to allocate 10% of the statutory after tax profits
的累計總額達到公司註冊資本的 to the statutory reserve until the cumulative total of
50%。待中國有關部門批准後， the reserve reaches 50% of the Company’s registered
法定儲備可用於抵銷任何累計虧 capital. Subject to the approval of the relevant PRC
損或增加公司的註冊資本。法定 authorities, the statutory reserve may be used to offset
儲備不可用於向中國附屬公司股 any accumulated losses or increase the registered capital
東分派股息。 of the companies. The statutory reserve is not available
for dividend distribution to shareholders of the PRC
subsidiaries.
(b) 資本儲備 (b) Capital reserve
本集團的資本儲備指最終控股公 The capital reserve of the Group represents the
司及股東的貢獻及代價超出所收 contribution from the ultimate holding company and
購非控股權益的賬面值的部分。 shareholders and the excess of the consideration over
the carrying amount of the non-controlling interests
acquired.
(c) 匯兌波動儲備 (c) Exchange fluctuation reserve
匯兌波動儲備指因換算其功能貨 The exchange fluctuation reserve represents exchange
幣與本集團呈列貨幣不同的海外 differences arising from the translation of the financial
業務的財務報表而產生的匯兌差 statements of foreign operations whose functional
額。 currencies are different from the Group’s presentation
currency.
(d) 股份支付儲備 (d) Share-based payment reserve

本集團的主要金融工具包括租賃負 The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise lease

長期銀行存款。該等金融工具的主要 balances and long-term bank deposits. The main purpose of
目的是為本集團的營運籌措資金。本 these financial instruments is to raise finance for the Group’s
集團的業務營運直接產生多種其他金 operations. The Group has various other financial assets and
融資產及負債，如貿易應收款項及應 liabilities such as trade and bills receivables and trade and bills
收票據以及貿易應付款項及應付票據。 payables, which arise directly from its operations.

險。董事會審閱及同意相關政策以管 of directors reviews and agrees policies for managing each of

資本管理 Capital management
本集團資本管理的主要目標為確保本 The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management are
集團持續經營的能力以及維持穩健的 to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern
資本比率，以支持其業務及為股東爭 and to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to support its
取最大價值。 business and maximise shareholders’ value.
本集團考慮經濟狀況變化及相關資產 The Group manages its capital structure and makes
的風險特徵來管理其資本架構並作出 adjustments to it in light of changes in economic conditions
相應調整。為維持或調整資本架構， and the risk characteristics of the underlying assets. To
本集團或會調整支付予股東的股息、 maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may adjust
退回股本予股東或發行新股。本集團 the dividend payment to shareholders, return capital to
不受任何外部資本限制的約束。年 shareholders or issue new shares. The Group is not subject to
內，本集團的資本管理目標、政策或 any externally imposed capital requirements. No changes were
程序未發生變化。 made in the objectives, policies or processes for managing
capital during the year.
本集團採用資本負債比率監控資本， The Group monitors capital using a gearing ratio, which is debt
該比率按債務除以經調整資本計算。 divided by the adjusted capital. Debt includes interest-bearing
債務包括計息借貸以及租賃負債。資 borrowings and lease liabilities. Capital represents equity
本指母公司擁有人應佔權益。於報告 attributable to owners of the parent. The gearing ratios as at

的公平值按具有類似條款、信貸風險 portion of lease liabilities have been calculated by discounting
及剩餘到期日的工具的現行利率折現 the expected future cash flows using rates currently available

工具公平值管理的政策及程序。企業 determining the policies and procedures for the fair value
融資團隊直接向財務總監及董事會匯 management of financial instruments. The corporate finance
報。於各報告期，企業融資團隊會分 team reports directly to the chief financial officer and the board

用的主要輸入數據。財務總監已審閱 analyses the movements in the values of financial instruments
並批准估值，且就年度財務報告與董 and determines the major inputs applied in the valuation.
事會討論估值過程及結果。 The valuation is reviewed and approved by the chief financial
officer. The valuation process and results are discussed with
the board of directors for annual financial reporting.
金融資產及負債的公平值按當前交易 The fair values of the financial assets and liabilities are included
（強制或清算出售除外）中雙方自願進 at the amount at which the instrument could be exchanged
行工具交換的金額入賬。以下為用於 in a current transaction between willing parties, other than
估計公平值的方法及假設。 in a forced or liquidation sale. The following methods and
assumptions were used to estimate the fair values.
本集團投資於非上市投資，即中國內 The Group invests in unlisted investments, which represent
地銀行發行的理財產品。本集團採用 wealth management products issued by banks in Mainland
折現現金流量估值模式，根據具類似 China. The Group has estimated the fair value of these unlisted
條款及風險的工具的市場利率，估計 investments by using a discounted cash flow valuation model
該等非上市投資的公允價值。 based on the market interest rates of instruments with similar
terms and risks.
